<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"fraction of variants at or below"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"ECDF"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">4. box plot</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">boxplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">""</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Box plot"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">5. violin</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">violinplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">inner</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"quart"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cut</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">""</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Violin plot"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">6. strip plot</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">stripplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">size</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">alpha</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.4</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">jitter</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.25</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">""</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Strip plot: every variant is a dot"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">7. count plot</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">countplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">""</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"number of variants"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Count plot"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><h3>Assemble a multi-panel figure and save it</h3><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">a three-panel figure</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axes</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">3</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">14</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>

<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">histplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="s2">"missense"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bins</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">40</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">])</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ecdfplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">hue</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">hue_order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">boxplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">order</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">TYPES</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">])</span>

<span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">letter</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"abc"</span><span class="p">):</span>
    <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_title</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">letter</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">loc</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"left"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">fontweight</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"bold"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"number of variants"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"cumulative fraction"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">""</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">tight_layout</span><span class="p">()</span>

<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">savefig</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">FIGS</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="s2">"score_distributions.png"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">dpi</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">300</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bbox_inches</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"tight"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">savefig</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">FIGS</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="s2">"score_distributions.pdf"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bbox_inches</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"tight"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><ul><li><strong>PNG</strong> at 300 dpi for slides and documents; <strong>PDF</strong> or <strong>SVG</strong> (vector, infinitely sharp) for papers and posters.</li><li><code>bbox_inches="tight"</code> trims the white border so labels are not clipped.</li><li>Use one palette and keep each category the same colour in every figure. Readers learn your colours.</li><li>The <code>colorblind</code> palette is a safe default; avoid red/green as the only distinction.</li></ul><aside class="q-callout is-why"><strong class="q-callout-title">Pick the plot after you pick the question</strong><p>Do not browse for a pretty chart. Write the sentence you want the reader to take away ("nonsense variants sit near zero; most missense variants do not") and choose the plot that makes that sentence obvious.</p></aside></div><div class="q-practice"><h3>Practice: your turn</h3><p>Reading code teaches you what code looks like; writing it teaches you to code. Do these in your notebook. Each has a starter, a <strong>check cell</strong> that stays silent when you are right and raises an error when you are not (read it: that is your Stage 2 skill at work), and a hidden solution. Tick <em>Done</em> when the check prints <code>all checks passed</code>.</p><div class="q-ex" data-ex="e9a"><div class="q-ex-head"><h4>Your turn: a plotting function</h4><label class="q-ex-done"><input type="checkbox" class="q-ex-check"> Done</label></div><div class="q-ex-task">Write <code>plot_ecdf_by(data, column, ax)</code> that draws one ECDF line per group of <code>column</code> onto the given <code>ax</code>, labels the x-axis, and <strong>returns the axes</strong>. Functions that take an <code>ax</code> can be dropped into any multi-panel figure.</div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">starter: copy into a cell and complete it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">plot_ecdf_by</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">column</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">):</span>
    <span class="k">pass</span>   <span class="c1"># TODO</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">check: run this after your code</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">()</span>
<span class="n">out</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plot_ecdf_by</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="n">out</span> <span class="ow">is</span> <span class="n">ax</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">lines</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mi">3</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">get_xlabel</span><span class="p">()</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="s2">""</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"all checks passed"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><details class="q-details q-ex-sol"><summary>Show a solution (try for ten minutes first)</summary><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one solution</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">plot_ecdf_by</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">column</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">):</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""One ECDF line per group, drawn onto ax."""</span>
    <span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">ecdfplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">hue</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">column</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"cumulative fraction"</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">ax</span></code></pre></div></details></div><div class="q-ex" data-ex="e9b"><div class="q-ex-head"><h4>Your turn: design your own figure</h4><label class="q-ex-done"><input type="checkbox" class="q-ex-check"> Done</label></div><div class="q-ex-task">Make a figure with two panels side by side: on the left a <strong>histogram</strong> of missense scores (30 bins); on the right a <strong>box plot</strong> of missense score by <code>region</code>. Label all axes, tidy the layout, and save it as <code>my_figure.png</code> in <code>results/figures</code> at 300 dpi. Choose colours you would be happy to show a supervisor.</div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">starter: copy into a cell and complete it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axes</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">11</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="c1"># TODO: left panel, right panel, labels, tight_layout, savefig</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">check: run this after your code</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">assert</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">FIGS</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="s2">"my_figure.png"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">exists</span><span class="p">()</span> <span class="ow">and</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">FIGS</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="s2">"my_figure.png"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">stat</span><span class="p">()</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">st_size</span> <span class="o">&gt;</span> <span class="mi">10_000</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">patches</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&gt;</span> <span class="mi">10</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">get_xlabel</span><span class="p">()</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="s2">""</span> <span class="ow">and</span> <span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">get_ylabel</span><span class="p">()</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="s2">""</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"all checks passed"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><details class="q-details q-ex-sol"><summary>Show a solution (try for ten minutes first)</summary><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one solution</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">miss</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="s2">"missense"</span><span class="p">]</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">axes</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">11</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">histplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bins</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">30</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">])</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">boxplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"region"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">])</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"number of variants"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"region"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">axes</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">tick_params</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">axis</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"x"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">rotation</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">30</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">tight_layout</span><span class="p">()</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">savefig</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">FIGS</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="s2">"my_figure.png"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">dpi</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">300</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">bbox_inches</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"tight"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div></details></div></div><div class="q-puzzles"><div class="q-puzzle" data-qid="s9p1" data-part="1"><div class="q-puzzle-head"><span class="q-star" aria-hidden="true">&#9733;</span><h4>First star</h4></div><div class="q-puzzle-body"><p>What is the <strong>median</strong> <code>score</code> of the <code>nonsense</code> variants, to 2 decimal places? This is the line in the middle of the nonsense box in your box plot.</p></div><div class="q-answer"><label class="q-prompt" for="in-s9p1">answer =</label><input id="in-s9p1" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" placeholder="2 decimal places"><button type="button" class="q-check">Check answer</button></div><p class="q-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><details class="q-details q-help"><summary>Stuck? Nudge and solution</summary><p>Filter to <code>record_type == "nonsense"</code>, take <code>["score"].median()</code>.</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one way to get it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">loc</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="s2">"nonsense"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">median</span><span class="p">(),</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">))</span></code></pre></div></details></div><div class="q-puzzle is-part2" data-qid="s9p2" data-part="2" hidden><div class="q-puzzle-head"><span class="q-star" aria-hidden="true">&#9733;</span><h4>Second star</h4></div><div class="q-puzzle-body"><p>The histogram of missense scores is rarely one tidy hump. Count the missense variants with a <code>score</code> <strong>strictly below 0.5</strong>.</p></div><div class="q-answer"><label class="q-prompt" for="in-s9p2">answer =</label><input id="in-s9p2" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" placeholder="a whole number"><button type="button" class="q-check">Check answer</button></div><p class="q-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><details class="q-details q-help"><summary>Stuck? Nudge and solution</summary><p>A boolean mask again: <code>(miss["score"] &lt; 0.5).sum()</code> adds up the <code>True</code> values.</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one way to get it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">miss</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="s2">"missense"</span><span class="p">]</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">&lt;</span> <span class="mf">0.5</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">())</span></code></pre></div></details></div></div></section>
<section class="q-stage" id="stage-10" data-stage="10"><header class="q-stage-head"><div class="q-stage-title"><span class="q-stage-num">Stage 9<span class="q-badge is-optional">Optional</span></span><h2>Heatmap Heights: plots for relationships and patterns</h2></div><div class="q-stage-stars" data-stars-for="10" aria-label="Stars earned in this stage"><span class="q-s" data-part="1">&#9733;</span><span class="q-s" data-part="2">&#9733;</span></div></header><p class="q-promise">Scatter plots, density views, error bars, a track along the sequence, and the heatmap that shows thousands of values at once. <span class="q-time">About 60 minutes.</span></p><p class="q-colab-row"><a class="q-colab" href="https://colab.research.google.com/drive/{% endraw %}{{ site.data.colab.nb4 }}{% raw %}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in Colab: Notebook 4: plots</a><span class="q-colab-note">Free Google account needed. Use File, Save a copy in Drive.</span></p><div class="q-prose"><p>Open notebook 4 again (this stage is its second half) and add <code>miss = df[df["record_type"] == "missense"]</code> if your session was reset. Distributions answered "what does it look like?". These plots answer "what goes with what?" and "where along the protein?".</p><h3>Two variables</h3><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">1. scatter</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">4.5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">scatterplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"se"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">s</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">8</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">alpha</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.4</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"standard error"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Do noisier measurements score differently?"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">2. trend line + correlation</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">4.5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">regplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"expts"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"se"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x_jitter</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.2</span><span class="p">,</span>
            <span class="n">scatter_kws</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span><span class="s2">"s"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"alpha"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mf">0.3</span><span class="p">},</span> <span class="n">line_kws</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span><span class="s2">"color"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"C3"</span><span class="p">},</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"number of experiments"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"standard error"</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"se"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"expts"</span><span class="p">]]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">corr</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">method</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"spearman"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">round</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><aside class="q-callout is-note"><strong class="q-callout-title">Correlation is a number; the plot is the check</strong><p>A correlation coefficient can look strong because of one cluster, or weak because the relationship is curved. Always look at the scatter that goes with it. Spearman (used above) compares ranks, so it is robust to outliers and to curved but steadily rising relationships.</p></aside><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">3. hexbin (many points)</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">4.5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">hb</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">hexbin</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"se"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">gridsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">30</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mincnt</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cmap</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"viridis"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">colorbar</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">hb</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"number of variants"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"standard error"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Hexbin: when dots overlap"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><h3>Show the uncertainty</h3><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">4. points with error bars</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">sub</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">between</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">75</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">76</span><span class="p">)]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sort_values</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"hgvs_pro"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">lower</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"lower_ci"</span><span class="p">])</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">clip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">lower</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span>   <span class="c1"># error bars must not be negative</span>
<span class="n">upper</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"upper_ci"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">])</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">clip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">lower</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">11</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">errorbar</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">)),</span> <span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">yerr</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">lower</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">upper</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">fmt</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"o"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">capsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ms</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_xticks</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">)),</span> <span class="n">sub</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"hgvs_pro"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">rotation</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">90</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">fontsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">8</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">axhline</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ls</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"--"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">color</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"grey"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">lw</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"Scores with 95</span><span class="si">% i</span><span class="s2">ntervals"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><aside class="q-callout is-trap"><strong class="q-callout-title"><code>ValueError: 'yerr' must not contain negative values</code></strong><p>Matplotlib wants error sizes, not endpoints, and sizes cannot be negative. That is why the code subtracts the score from the interval ends. Read the message, think about what you passed, fix the arithmetic.</p></aside><h3>A track along the sequence</h3><p>Position on the x-axis, a summary on the y-axis, and shaded bands for the annotated regions. This is the plot that connects a table to the structure of the thing it came from.</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">5. line track with region bands</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">by_position</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span>
    <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">groupby</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">)[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span>
        <span class="o">.</span><span class="n">agg</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">n_subs</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"size"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mean_score</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">)</span>
        <span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reset_index</span><span class="p">()</span>
<span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">x</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">by_position</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">astype</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">smooth</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">by_position</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">rolling</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">center</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">True</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_periods</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span>

<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">12</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">3.8</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">r</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">colour</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">zip</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">regions</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">itertuples</span><span class="p">(),</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"C0"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"C1"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"C2"</span><span class="p">]):</span>
    <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">axvspan</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">r</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">start</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">r</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">end</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">color</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">colour</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">alpha</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.12</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">text</span><span class="p">((</span><span class="n">r</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">start</span> <span class="o">+</span> <span class="n">r</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">end</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">/</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">1.28</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">r</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">region</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ha</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"center"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">fontsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">9</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">plot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">by_position</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">lw</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">0.8</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">color</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"grey"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean per position"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">plot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">x</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">smooth</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">lw</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">color</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"black"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">label</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"5-position rolling mean"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlim</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">403</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">ylim</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">1.35</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean missense score"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">legend</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">loc</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"lower right"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">frameon</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">False</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><h3>The heatmap</h3><p>One cell per substitution: position along the x-axis, the new letters on the y-axis, colour for the score. Thousands of numbers become one picture where stripes mean "this position hates any change" and horizontal bands mean "this substitution is bad everywhere".</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">6. heatmap</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">AA</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"Ala"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Val"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Ile"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Leu"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Met"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Phe"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Trp"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Tyr"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Cys"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Gly"</span><span class="p">,</span>
      <span class="s2">"Pro"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Ser"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Thr"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Asn"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Gln"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Asp"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Glu"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"His"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Lys"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"Arg"</span><span class="p">]</span>

<span class="n">grid</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pivot_table</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">index</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"alt"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">columns</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">values</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reindex</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">AA</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">14</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_facecolor</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"#dddddd"</span><span class="p">)</span>      <span class="c1"># untested substitutions show as grey</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">heatmap</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">grid</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cmap</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"viridis"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">vmin</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">vmax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">1.2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">xticklabels</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">25</span><span class="p">,</span>
            <span class="n">cbar_kws</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span><span class="s2">"label"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"abundance score"</span><span class="p">},</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"new letters"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><aside class="q-callout is-tip"><strong class="q-callout-title">Make the heatmap readable</strong><ul><li>Fix <code>vmin</code>/<code>vmax</code> so colours mean the same thing across plots.</li><li>Order the rows meaningfully (here by chemical similarity) instead of alphabetically.</li><li>Use a perceptually even colormap like <code>viridis</code>; avoid rainbow maps that invent edges.</li><li>Show missing cells honestly, in grey, rather than as zero.</li></ul></aside><h3>Plot hygiene checklist</h3><ul><li>Axis labels with units; a title that states the finding, not just the variables.</li><li>Same colour = same category in every figure.</li><li>Text readable at the size it will be printed (14 to 16 pt at final size is a good aim).</li><li>Save from code, with a descriptive filename, into <code>results/figures/</code>.</li><li>Show the raw data points where you can; summaries alone hide too much.</li></ul></div><div class="q-practice"><h3>Practice: your turn</h3><p>Reading code teaches you what code looks like; writing it teaches you to code. Do these in your notebook. Each has a starter, a <strong>check cell</strong> that stays silent when you are right and raises an error when you are not (read it: that is your Stage 2 skill at work), and a hidden solution. Tick <em>Done</em> when the check prints <code>all checks passed</code>.</p><div class="q-ex" data-ex="e10a"><div class="q-ex-head"><h4>Your turn: window_means <span class="q-badge is-stretch">Stretch</span></h4><label class="q-ex-done"><input type="checkbox" class="q-ex-check"> Done</label></div><div class="q-ex-task">Generalise the 10-position windows from the puzzle. Write <code>window_means(miss, size=10)</code> returning a DataFrame with columns <code>window</code>, <code>n</code> and <code>mean</code> (mean missense score), one row per window of <code>size</code> positions.</div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">starter: copy into a cell and complete it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">window_means</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">size</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">10</span><span class="p">):</span>
    <span class="k">pass</span>   <span class="c1"># TODO</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">check: run this after your code</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">w10</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">window_means</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">w25</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">window_means</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">size</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">25</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">list</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">w10</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">columns</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"window"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"n"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">]</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">w25</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&lt;</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">w10</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="n">w10</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"n"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sum</span><span class="p">()</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="nb">len</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">w25</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"window"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">%</span> <span class="mi">25</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">all</span><span class="p">()</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"all checks passed"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><details class="q-details q-ex-sol"><summary>Show a solution (try for ten minutes first)</summary><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one solution</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">window_means</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">size</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">10</span><span class="p">):</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""Mean missense score in windows of `size` positions."""</span>
    <span class="n">windows</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">//</span> <span class="n">size</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="n">size</span>
    <span class="n">out</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">groupby</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">windows</span><span class="p">)[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">agg</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">n</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"size"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mean</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="n">out</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">index</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">name</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="s2">"window"</span>
    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">out</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reset_index</span><span class="p">()</span></code></pre></div></details></div><div class="q-ex" data-ex="e10b"><div class="q-ex-head"><h4>Your turn: zoom in on a stretch <span class="q-badge is-stretch">Stretch</span></h4><label class="q-ex-done"><input type="checkbox" class="q-ex-check"> Done</label></div><div class="q-ex-task">Draw the heatmap again, but only for <strong>positions 100 to 140</strong>, with the new letters in the order of <code>AA</code>. Store the pivoted table in <code>grid_zoom</code> and plot it with <code>annot=False</code> and tick labels on every position. Zooming in is how you turn a pattern into a story.</div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">starter: copy into a cell and complete it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">grid_zoom</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="kc">None</span>   <span class="c1"># TODO: filter positions, pivot_table, reindex(AA)</span>
<span class="c1"># TODO: sns.heatmap(...)</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">check: run this after your code</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">assert</span> <span class="n">grid_zoom</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="mi">20</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="mi">1</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="n">grid_zoom</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">shape</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">41</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">min</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">c</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">c</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="n">grid_zoom</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">columns</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mi">100</span> <span class="ow">and</span> <span class="nb">max</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">c</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">c</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="n">grid_zoom</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">columns</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">140</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"all checks passed"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><details class="q-details q-ex-sol"><summary>Show a solution (try for ten minutes first)</summary><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one solution</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">zoom</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">between</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">100</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">140</span><span class="p">)]</span>
<span class="n">grid_zoom</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">zoom</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">pivot_table</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">index</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"alt"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">columns</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">values</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">reindex</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">AA</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">12</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">4.5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set_facecolor</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"#dddddd"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">heatmap</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">grid_zoom</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">cmap</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"viridis"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">vmin</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">0</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">vmax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mf">1.2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">xticklabels</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div></details></div><div class="q-ex" data-ex="e10c"><div class="q-ex-head"><h4>Your turn: does noisier mean lower? <span class="q-badge is-stretch">Stretch</span></h4><label class="q-ex-done"><input type="checkbox" class="q-ex-check"> Done</label></div><div class="q-ex-task">For each position compute the mean missense <code>score</code> and the mean <code>se</code> (put them in a DataFrame <code>by_pos</code> with columns <code>mean_score</code> and <code>mean_se</code>). Draw a scatter of one against the other, and store the <strong>Spearman correlation</strong> between the two columns in <code>rho</code>. Then write one sentence in a Markdown cell: what does the plot say?</div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">starter: copy into a cell and complete it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">by_pos</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="kc">None</span>   <span class="c1"># TODO</span>
<span class="n">rho</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="kc">None</span>      <span class="c1"># TODO</span></code></pre></div><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">check: run this after your code</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">list</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">columns</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"mean_se"</span><span class="p">]</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="o">-</span><span class="mi">1</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="n">rho</span> <span class="o">&lt;=</span> <span class="mi">1</span>
<span class="k">assert</span> <span class="nb">abs</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">rho</span> <span class="o">-</span> <span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">corr</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_se"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">method</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"spearman"</span><span class="p">))</span> <span class="o">&lt;</span> <span class="mf">1e-12</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"all checks passed"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><details class="q-details q-ex-sol"><summary>Show a solution (try for ten minutes first)</summary><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one solution</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">by_pos</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">groupby</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">)</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">agg</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">mean_score</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">mean_se</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"se"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">rho</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">corr</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean_se"</span><span class="p">],</span> <span class="n">method</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"spearman"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">fig</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">plt</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">subplots</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">figsize</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mf">5.5</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">4.5</span><span class="p">))</span>
<span class="n">sns</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">scatterplot</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">by_pos</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">x</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean_se"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">y</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">s</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">14</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">ax</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">ax</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">set</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">xlabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean standard error"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">ylabel</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean score"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">title</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="sa">f</span><span class="s2">"Spearman rho = </span><span class="si">{</span><span class="n">rho</span><span class="si">:</span><span class="s2">.2f</span><span class="si">}</span><span class="s2">"</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div></details></div></div><div class="q-puzzles"><div class="q-puzzle" data-qid="s10p1" data-part="1"><div class="q-puzzle-head"><span class="q-star" aria-hidden="true">&#9733;</span><h4>First star</h4></div><div class="q-puzzle-body"><p>Using only <code>missense</code> rows, group by the new letters (<code>alt</code>) and take the mean <code>score</code>. Which new amino acid, written as its three letters like <code>Ala</code>, has the <strong>lowest</strong> mean? It is the dimmest horizontal band in your heatmap.</p></div><div class="q-answer"><label class="q-prompt" for="in-s10p1">answer =</label><input id="in-s10p1" type="text" inputmode="text" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" placeholder="Xxx"><button type="button" class="q-check">Check answer</button></div><p class="q-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><details class="q-details q-help"><summary>Stuck? Nudge and solution</summary><p><code>miss.groupby("alt")["score"].mean().idxmin()</code>. Remember <code>miss</code> must only contain missense rows, otherwise <code>Ter</code> and <code>=</code> sneak in.</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one way to get it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">groupby</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"alt"</span><span class="p">)[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">mean</span><span class="p">()</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">idxmin</span><span class="p">())</span></code></pre></div></details></div><div class="q-puzzle is-part2" data-qid="s10p2" data-part="2" hidden><div class="q-puzzle-head"><span class="q-star" aria-hidden="true">&#9733;</span><h4>Second star</h4></div><div class="q-puzzle-body"><p>Cut the sequence into windows of 10 positions: <code>window = position // 10 * 10</code>, so positions 120 to 129 form window <code>120</code>. Among windows that contain <strong>at least 30 missense variants</strong>, which window has the lowest mean missense <code>score</code>? Answer with the window's starting number.</p></div><div class="q-answer"><label class="q-prompt" for="in-s10p2">answer =</label><input id="in-s10p2" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" placeholder="a multiple of 10"><button type="button" class="q-check">Check answer</button></div><p class="q-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><details class="q-details q-help"><summary>Stuck? Nudge and solution</summary><p>Make a <code>window</code> column, <code>groupby("window")</code> with <code>n=("score", "size")</code> and <code>mean=("score", "mean")</code>, filter on <code>n &gt;= 30</code>, then <code>idxmin()</code> of the mean.</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">one way to get it</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">miss</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">assign</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">window</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">miss</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"position"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">//</span> <span class="mi">10</span> <span class="o">*</span> <span class="mi">10</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="n">w</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">miss</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">groupby</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"window"</span><span class="p">)[</span><span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">agg</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">n</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"size"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">mean</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">w</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">w</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"n"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="mi">30</span><span class="p">][</span><span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">idxmin</span><span class="p">())</span></code></pre></div></details></div></div></section>
<section class="q-stage" id="stage-11" data-stage="11"><header class="q-stage-head"><div class="q-stage-title"><span class="q-stage-num">Stage 10<span class="q-badge is-core">Core</span></span><h2>The Summit: does your conclusion survive a different rule?</h2></div><div class="q-stage-stars" data-stars-for="11" aria-label="Stars earned in this stage"><span class="q-s" data-part="1">&#9733;</span><span class="q-s" data-part="2">&#9733;</span><span class="q-s" data-part="3">&#9733;</span></div></header><p class="q-promise">A result is only as solid as the choices behind it. You will change one rule, measure how much the answer moves, and write down what you conclude. <span class="q-time">About 40 minutes.</span></p><p class="q-colab-row"><a class="q-colab" href="https://colab.research.google.com/drive/{% endraw %}{{ site.data.colab.nb5 }}{% raw %}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in Colab: Notebook 5: sensitivity and conclusions</a><span class="q-colab-note">Free Google account needed. Use File, Save a copy in Drive.</span></p><div class="q-prose"><p>Every analysis contains judgement calls: which rows to keep, how to score, where to cut. A trustworthy analysis shows how much the answer <em>depends</em> on them. Take one call you have already made implicitly: <strong>should we trust variants that were measured in only a couple of experiments?</strong></p><h3>Turn the rule into a dial</h3><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">cell</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="k">def</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">summarise</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">region</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"phosphatase_domain"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_expts</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">):</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="sd">"""Count and mean of missense scores in one region.</span>

<span class="sd">    min_expts: keep only variants measured in at least this many experiments</span>
<span class="sd">    (None keeps everything).</span>
<span class="sd">    """</span>
    <span class="n">keep</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"record_type"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="s2">"missense"</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="o">&amp;</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"region"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">==</span> <span class="n">region</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="k">if</span> <span class="n">min_expts</span> <span class="ow">is</span> <span class="ow">not</span> <span class="kc">None</span><span class="p">:</span>
        <span class="n">keep</span> <span class="o">&amp;=</span> <span class="n">df</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"expts"</span><span class="p">]</span> <span class="o">&gt;=</span> <span class="n">min_expts</span>
    <span class="k">return</span> <span class="n">df</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">loc</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="n">keep</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"score"</span><span class="p">]</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">agg</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="s2">"count"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">])</span>

<span class="n">summarise</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">),</span> <span class="n">summarise</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_expts</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="mi">3</span><span class="p">)</span></code></pre></div><p>Now sweep the dial and look at the whole curve, not one cherry-picked value:</p><div class="qcode"><div class="qcode-bar"><span class="qcode-name">cell</span><button class="qcopy" type="button">Copy</button></div><pre><code><span class="n">rows</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">[]</span>
<span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">k</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="p">[</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">2</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">3</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">6</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">7</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">8</span><span class="p">]:</span>
    <span class="n">s</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">summarise</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">df</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">min_expts</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">k</span><span class="p">)</span>
    <span class="n">rows</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">append</span><span class="p">({</span><span class="s2">"min_expts"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="n">k</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s2">"n_variants"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="nb">int</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">s</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"count"</span><span class="p">]),</span> <span class="s2">"mean_score"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="n">s</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"mean"</span><span class="p">]})</span>
<span class="n">sweep</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">pd</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">DataFrame</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">rows</span><span class="p">)</span>
